Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the reactors space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 164 and the convergence rate is O(174/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on reactors structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 164. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 174. QED.
